### 2.2. Missing implementation required before apply

The repository baseline does not contain:

- `personnel_orchestration_operations` and its exact idempotency constraints;
- ADR-065 assignment provenance/version columns and guards;
- `application_assignment_key` and its exact partial uniqueness scopes;
- the current-active-primary partial unique index and non-VOID period exclusion constraint;
- the complete ADR-065 event columns, coupling guards, strict success-audit vocabulary,
  or operation-owned import provenance contract;
- `person_assignment_boundary_runs`, its SECURITY DEFINER lifecycle functions/runtime
  role, and delivery-attempt/ACK/onboarding retry persistence prerequisites;
- the ADR-048 caller-owned Person Shell create/link/identity write port;
- strict `execute_assignment_command_tx(conn, command)` in C2;
- `reconcile_employee_primary_assignment_tx(...)`;
- expected-state token generation/locked revalidation;
- atomic repair application service and operator apply API;
- operator UI for `+ Из контр. списка`.

Existing wrappers such as `reconcile_employee_primary_assignment` own their transactions
or perform post-transaction reads and therefore cannot be called from the composite.
Existing C2 helpers include fallback/default behavior and cannot be exposed as the strict
ADR-065 port without remediation.

## 3. Scope and non-goals

### 3.1. In scope

This WP prepares and implements the approved composite repair in bounded slices:

1. preserve and harden read-only discovery/preflight;
2. add exact expected-state and preview-token behavior;
3. add operation/idempotency and database hard guarantees;
4. expose caller-owned ADR-048, C2 and reconciliation ports without duplicating authority;
5. execute atomic composite P0 repair;
6. persist exact events, strict audit, provenance and import binding;
7. expose the operator API/application service;
8. add the operator UI at `/directory/personnel/journal`;
9. complete the writer inventory/rollout gate before apply activation.

P1 remains a static server classification throughout. The common implementation must not
turn P1 into P0, `LINK_ONLY`, or a name-based create. Initial production activation may be
limited to P0 only; P1 apply remains disabled until the same transaction, concurrency and
fault-injection proof passes for its adoption branch.

### 3.2. Explicit non-goals

- no manual SQL repair or mass repair of production data;
- no change to `GET /api/ppr/employees/{employee_id}` or its resolver/error mapping;
- no automatic Person merge, rehire, HIRE, transfer, absence or termination inference;
- no change to standalone `LINK_ONLY` or `OPEN_MISSING_ASSIGNMENT` semantics;
- no assignment writes outside ADR-043/C2;
- no Person matching rules outside ADR-048;
- no use of import rows, Employee projection, HIRE events or personnel orders as assignment
  decisions;
- no committed intermediate Employee→Person link without the required assignment;
- no feature activation before the common writer and schema rollout gates pass.

---

## 4. Production evidence as an acceptance scenario

Production evidence is a test scenario, not a source of operator decisions:

| Employee | Observed facts | ADR-065 classification |
|---:|---|---|
| `46` | active Employee; one active exact Employee IIN; `person_id=NULL`; no Person for that IIN; no current PersonAssignment; PPR GET returns normal `409` | `E1 + L0 + P0` |
| `47` | same state shape | `E1 + L0 + P0` |
| `48` | same state shape | `E1 + L0 + P0` |

No full IIN is recorded in this WP. Names and current Employee projection fields are not
assignment input. For each employee the only successful target is:

```text
EXISTING_CARD_REPAIR
  / LINK_AND_OPEN_MISSING_ASSIGNMENT
  / S_UNLINKED_NO_PRIMARY_P0_WITH_INTENT
  → EMPLOYEE_PERSON_AND_ASSIGNMENT_REPAIRED
```

The operation may proceed only after an authorized operator separately supplies and
confirms all eight decisions: org unit, position, rate, employment type,
`is_primary=true`, start date, evidence/admissibility, and controlled reason. The factual
P0 state does not fill any of them.

## 5. Implementation slices

### Slice 0 — Freeze the read-only discovery/preflight contract

**Purpose.** Preserve the already implemented exact-IIN discovery as the only classifier
entry point while later slices are built.

**Existing files to modify only if implementation review finds normative drift:**

- `app/directory/personnel_lk_routes.py`
- `app/directory/personnel_lk_schemas.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_preflight_service.py`
- `app/services/adr048_person_resolution_service.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/personnel_order_evidence_fingerprint.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_preflight_api.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr048_person_resolution_service.py`

**Inputs.** Exact 12-ASCII-digit IIN; optional discovery import selection; complete
assignment intent when classification is requested.

**Transaction.** One caller-owned `REPEATABLE READ READ ONLY`; transaction settings before
the first business SELECT; rollback on every exit.

**Outputs.** Safe Employee/Person/assignment/import projections, blockers, P0/P1 preview,
`preflight_complete`, and literal `apply_available=false`.

**Fail closed.** Invalid IIN, Employee/IIN conflict, Person ambiguity/incompatibility,
existing link, existing non-VOID primary, import ownership conflict, unavailable/current
watermark failure, incomplete intent, invalid reason/evidence.

**Tests.** Retain API-stack permission, validation redaction, no-name fallback, P0/P1,
provenance, evidence, watermark and write-free proofs.

**Ready when.** Existing tests pass from a clean baseline and the response remains incapable
of authorizing apply.

### Slice 1 — Expected-state closure and preview token

**Purpose.** Implement ADR-065 §7 without enabling writes. This is the recommended first
limited implementation slice after this WP is approved.

**Exact two-step read-only wire flow.** ADR-065 §§7–8 requires the complete confirmed P0
Shell-create intention in the trusted business digest while the identity fingerprint and
idempotency input-binding verifier are server-derived and never authoritative client
values. The implementation therefore uses exactly these two POST routes; there is no
alternative one-step token path:

1. `POST /directory/personnel/lk/control-list-repair/preflight/prepare` accepts
   `ControlListRepairPreviewPrepareIn` and returns
   `ControlListRepairPreviewPrepareOut`. It classifies from one read-only snapshot and
   returns the exact non-secret confirmation material needed by the operator/client.
2. `POST /directory/personnel/lk/control-list-repair/preflight` accepts
   `ControlListRepairPreviewConfirmIn`. It repeats the read-only discovery, recreates every
   server-derived value, compares the opaque confirmation echo in constant time, closes
   both digests, and only then returns `ControlListRepairPreflightOut` with
   `personnel-preview-v1`.

Both requests contain the same client-entered business object: transient exact `iin`;
transient raw `idempotency_key`; UUID `correlation_id`; exact optional
`import_selection={batch_id,row_id,normalized_record_ids[]}`; optional discovery or complete
eight-decision `assignment_intent`; and, for P0, client-entered non-secret
`person_shell_facts={full_name,last_name,first_name,middle_name,birth_date}`. The eight
decisions are the confirmed org-unit tuple, confirmed position tuple, exact decimal rate,
C2 employment type, literal `is_primary=true`, start date, closed evidence union and
controlled reason, including the ADR verifier-confirmation tuple. P1 requires
`person_shell_facts=null`. Discovery may omit import selection, assignment intent and
Shell facts, but then step 1 returns requirements only and step 2 cannot issue a token.

The client cannot send top-level or nested authoritative `classification`, `branch`,
`mode`, `outcome`, `proposed_outcome`, target Person/Employee hashes, candidate decision,
watermark, expected-state collection, digest (including `assignment_intent_digest`), token
metadata, identity fingerprint or identity-input verifier. Unknown/extra fields are
forbidden. The raw IIN and idempotency
key are used transiently by the server and never returned.

The prepare response contains the ordinary safe preflight projection plus:

```text
confirmation_material.encoding = base64url-jcs-utf8-no-padding
confirmation_material.echo
confirmation_material.assignment_intent_profile_id = adr065-assignment-intent-v1
confirmation_material.assignment_intent_digest
confirmation_material.display.assignment_intent_summary
confirmation_material.display.person_shell_create_intention
confirmation_material.display.identity_type
confirmation_material.display.identity_fingerprint_profile_id
confirmation_material.display.identity_fingerprint_key_id
confirmation_material.display.identity_input_binding_profile_id
confirmation_material.display.identity_input_binding_key_id
confirmation_material.display.fio_normalization_profile_id
confirmation_material.display.correlation_id
confirmation_material.display.target_employee_id
operator_confirmation_requirements[]
authoritative_preview.{classification,branch,mode,proposed_outcome}
```

`confirmation_material.echo` is unpadded base64url of the UTF-8 RFC-8785 JCS bytes of the
closed object `confirmation_schema_version=adr065-preview-confirmation-v1`, actor user,
authorization-role/context fingerprint, environment, correlation, target Employee,
sorted import selection, `assignment_intent_profile_id=adr065-assignment-intent-v1`,
`assignment_intent_digest`, authoritative classification/branch/mode/outcome, and the
exact P0 `person_shell_create_intention` (or JSON null for P1). The intention has precisely
`schema_version=adr048-person-shell-create-v2`, `identity_type=IIN`, the profile/kid/
fingerprint triple, the binding profile/kid/verifier triple,
`fio_normalization_profile_id`, authoritative FIO/birth facts, and fixed
`source=enrollment`, `match_key_scheme=iin`, `person_status=active`.

The server computes `assignment_intent_digest` as lowercase hexadecimal
`SHA-256(ASCII(adr065-assignment-intent-v1) || 0x00 || UTF8(JCS(A)))`, where `0x00` is
one NUL octet (not the four printable characters `\x00`) and `A` is the following
closed object and every member is present before RFC-8785 canonicalization:

```text
assignment_intent_schema_version = adr065-assignment-intent-v1
org_unit = {
  org_unit_id: positive decimal-string,
  org_unit_normalized_stable_code: non-empty exact trimmed ASCII string <=128,
  operator_confirmed_normalized_org_name: normalized Unicode string <=512
}
position = {
  position_id: positive decimal-string,
  operator_confirmed_normalized_position_name: normalized Unicode string <=512
}
rate = canonical non-exponent decimal string, NUMERIC(4,2), 0 < rate <= 1.5
employment_type = primary | part_time | internal_combo | external | locum
is_primary = true
start_date = YYYY-MM-DD
evidence = {
  evidence_type: PERSONNEL_ORDER | EXTERNAL_REFERENCE,
  personnel_order_id: positive decimal-string | null,
  evidence_record_id: positive decimal-string | null,
  evidence_profile_id: adr065-po-evidence | null,
  evidence_profile_version: 1 | null,
  evidence_key_id: ASCII ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}$ | null,
  evidence_fingerprint: 64 lowercase hex,
  admissibility_confirmed: true
}
reason_code = one exact §6.2 ASCII value compatible with the server-derived row
verifier_confirmation = {
  verifier_user_id: positive decimal-string,
  confirmation_at: YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ,
  confirmation_reference: ASCII ^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:/-]{0,127}$
}
```

For `PERSONNEL_ORDER` the two IDs and three profile/key members are non-null; for
`EXTERNAL_REFERENCE` both IDs and all three profile/key members are JSON null. No other
member is nullable for a complete composite P0/P1 confirmation. JSON numbers are used
only for literal profile version `1`; IDs and rate remain the canonical strings required
by ADR §§6 and 8.1. Strings are neither trimmed nor case-folded during digesting. The
digest is not secret and cannot replace any identity HMAC; it binds only the already
authorized operator-intent object. Display renders the eight decision labels/IDs and only
non-secret P0 facts/profile IDs. The opaque echo necessarily carries the server-generated
identity fingerprint and input-binding verifier inside the exact Shell intention so that
confirm can return them byte-for-byte; both are domain-separated HMAC outputs under
independent 256-bit keys and disclose no practical offline IIN test without a key. Raw
IIN, idempotency key, HMAC secret, protected expected-state value and evidence protected
column never appear in display or echo, and no fingerprint/verifier appears in token,
logs, traces or metric labels.

Step 2 repeats every client-entered field and adds exactly
`confirmation_material_echo`. The server reconstructs `A` from the final confirm request,
recomputes `assignment_intent_digest`, rereads state and authorization context, derives
the four secret-backed profile outputs again, rebuilds the exact JCS bytes and encoded
echo, and performs a constant-time byte comparison. It never adopts an echoed digest,
fingerprint, verifier, classification, mode, branch or outcome. A change to any one of the
eight decisions, actor/context/target/correlation/import binding, P0 intention or derived
state therefore changes the reconstructed echo. Missing/malformed/extra/reordered encoded
material returns `PREVIEW_CONFIRMATION_MALFORMED`; a changed byte or recomputed-value
difference returns `PREVIEW_CONFIRMATION_MISMATCH`; changed business state uses the more
specific ADR stale/structural blocker. Only after equality may the server compute
`request_digest`, `expected_state_digest` and sign `personnel-preview-v1`. No token or
digest is returned on a failure path.

`reason_code` is a strict Literal of exactly:
`ACTIVE_ENROLLMENT_CONFIRMED`, `CONSISTENT_STATE_VERIFIED`,
`EXISTING_CARD_PERSON_LINK_GAP_CONFIRMED`,
`EXISTING_CARD_PERSON_AND_ASSIGNMENT_GAP_CONFIRMED`,
`MISSING_PRIMARY_ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED`,
`ERRONEOUS_ASSIGNMENT_RECORD_CONFIRMED`,
`REAL_LIFECYCLE_EPISODE_COMPLETION_CONFIRMED`,
`CURRENT_ASSIGNMENT_CHANGE_CONFIRMED`,
`FUTURE_ASSIGNMENT_PRESERVATION_CONFIRMED`, and
`FUTURE_ASSIGNMENT_CHANGE_CONFIRMED`. The complete ADR compatibility matrix is validated
server-side; composite P0/P1 accepts
`EXISTING_CARD_PERSON_AND_ASSIGNMENT_GAP_CONFIRMED`. Mode-as-reason, technical metadata,
free text and an eleventh value are rejected.

**Existing files:**

- `app/directory/personnel_lk_routes.py`
- `app/directory/personnel_lk_schemas.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_preflight_service.py`
- `app/config.py`
- `app/main.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_preflight_api.py`

**New files:**

- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_expected_state.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_confirmation.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_preview_token.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/infrastructure/personnel_preview_key_ring.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/infrastructure/person_iin_fingerprint_key_ring.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/infrastructure/idempotency_iin_binding_key_ring.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/infrastructure/expected_state_sensitive_hmac_key_ring.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/infrastructure/adr065_crypto_providers.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/adr065_readiness_service.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_expected_state.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_confirmation.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_preview_token.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_personnel_preview_key_ring.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_crypto_profile_separation.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_readiness.py`

**Server-derived state.** The service alone derives
`operation=EXISTING_CARD_REPAIR`, `classification=P0|P1`,
`branch=P0_CREATE|P1_ADOPT`, mode/outcome, target Employee/Person,
candidate-resolution result, row versions/existence facts, independently computed
business date and current watermark, evidence-scope generation, and every member of the
finite ADR §7.1 state: schema version/manifest hash, persons, employees,
employee-identities, assignments, assignment links, org units, positions, the four order
evidence collections and their scopes, import batches/rows/normalized records, and
Contacts. Empty P0 candidate collections and the exact Shell-create intention are explicit
members, not omissions. The server derives both digests and token metadata only after the
snapshot is closed.

**Final response.** Extend `ControlListRepairPreflightOut` with nullable
`preview_token`, literal `preview_token_version=personnel-preview-v1`,
`preview_token_kid`, `preview_issued_at`, `preview_expires_at`,
`request_digest`, `expected_state_digest`, and safe
`expected_state={state_schema_version,schema_manifest_hash,watermark,collection_counts}`.
It also returns the authoritative classification/branch/mode/proposed outcome, exact
`operator_confirmation_requirements`, blockers, and `preflight_complete`. Token and
digest fields are emitted only for a complete, current, ready preview.
`apply_available` remains `Literal[False]` for every response. The complete state
object remains server-side.

The opaque token uses exactly two unpadded base64url segments and its signed JCS payload
has exactly `token_version`, `kid`, `issued_at`, `expires_at`,
`actor_user_id`, `operation_type`, `mode`, nullable `target_employee_id`,
`request_digest`, and `expected_state_digest`. Classification, branch, outcome,
correlation and import IDs are bound through the request digest and are not extra payload
members. IDs are canonical decimal strings, digests are 64 lowercase hex, and timestamps
are exact UTC seconds.

**Digest and canonicalization.** RFC 8785 JCS over UTF-8 is used without trimming,
case-folding, float conversion, or omitted-null shortcuts. `request_digest` is lowercase
SHA-256 of the JCS object with every following member present in exactly this ADR §8.1
schema order; an inapplicable scalar is JSON null:

```text
request_schema_version, operation, mode, correlation_id,
actor_user_id, verifier_user_id, confirmation_at, confirmation_reference,
target_employee_id, target_employee_expected_state_hash,
target_person_id, target_person_expected_state_hash, person_shell_create_intention,
identity_expected_state_hash,
identity_input_binding_profile_id, identity_input_binding_key_id,
identity_input_binding_verifier,
import_batch_ids, import_row_ids, normalized_record_ids,
identity_type, identity_fingerprint_profile_id, identity_fingerprint_key_id,
identity_fingerprint,
original_assignment_id, original_assignment_expected_version,
current_assignment_id, current_assignment_expected_version,
future_assignment_id, future_assignment_expected_version,
replacement_target_assignment_id, replacement_target_expected_version,
personnel_order_id, evidence_record_id, evidence_type,
evidence_profile_id, evidence_profile_version, evidence_key_id, evidence_fingerprint,
org_unit_id, org_unit_normalized_stable_code, operator_confirmed_normalized_org_name,
position_id, operator_confirmed_normalized_position_name,
employment_type, rate, is_primary,
start_date, end_date, old_end_date, transition_date,
replacement_start_date, replacement_end_date,
changed_fields, reason_code
```

The server derives mode, target IDs/hashes, intention and all fingerprints/verifiers; the
client-entered confirmation fields merely supply the operator facts. Authorization context
is recomputed and binds operation lookup plus the P0 input-binding verifier exactly as ADR
§8.1; it is not added as a non-normative digest member. The raw idempotency key scopes
operation lookup through `idempotency_key_fingerprint` and the binding verifier but never
appears in JCS. Generated `operation_id` is absent.
`expected_state_digest` covers the complete §7.1 object with exactly
`state_schema_version`, `schema_manifest_hash`, `watermark`, `persons`, `employees`,
`employee_identities`, `person_assignments`, `employee_assignment_links`, `org_units`,
`positions`, the four personnel-order collections, `personnel_order_evidence_scopes`,
`hr_import_batches`, `hr_import_rows`, `hr_import_normalized_records`, and `contacts`.
Collection rows
and IDs use the ADR sort rules and every physical column is canonically typed. Generated
IDs are absent. Full IIN, raw identity, protected values and key material are
HMAC-bound under their ADR profiles but never enter token payload, logs, metrics, traces,
or observability labels.

**Four independent crypto providers.** `app/config.py`, `app/main.py` and
`adr065_crypto_providers.py` wire four immutable, environment-bound, service-account-readable
JSON key rings; no provider may consult another provider or fall back to application,
JWT/session/encryption/evidence secrets:

| Purpose/profile | Sole provider | Exact setting | Active/retained behavior |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PERSONNEL_PREVIEW_HMAC` / `personnel-preview-v1` | `personnel_preview_key_ring.py` | `ADR065_PERSONNEL_PREVIEW_HMAC_KEY_RING_FILE` | one ACTIVE signing key; previous key verifies only until `verify_until=verification_only_at+2100s`, then becomes RETIRED; unknown/retired kid fails |
| operational identity / `adr065-person-iin-fp-v1` | `person_iin_fingerprint_key_ring.py` | `ADR065_PERSON_IIN_FINGERPRINT_KEY_RING_FILE` | one ACTIVE derivation key; token-bound SCHEDULED/ACTIVE/VERIFICATION_ONLY/REVOKED/DESTROYED semantics exactly as ADR §8.1 |
| replay binding / `adr065-idempotency-iin-binding-v1` | `idempotency_iin_binding_key_ring.py` | `ADR065_IDEMPOTENCY_IIN_BINDING_KEY_RING_FILE` | separate one ACTIVE derivation key; retained verification keys cover every nonterminal operation and committed replay contract; no operational-IIN key reuse |
| protected §7.1 fields / `adr065-expected-state-sensitive-v1` | `expected_state_sensitive_hmac_key_ring.py` | `ADR065_EXPECTED_STATE_HMAC_KEY_RING_FILE` | one ACTIVE derivation key; previous verification-only keys retained at least 2100s so a live preview can be reproduced; never substitutes the order-evidence ring |

All four settings name separate absolute files. Each file is strict UTF-8 without BOM and
has exactly five top-level members: `schema_version`, `profile_id`, `profile_version`,
`environment_id`, `keys`. The first is literal `adr065-key-ring-file-v1`;
`profile_version` is JSON integer `1`; `profile_id` is the profile-specific const below;
`environment_id` byte-equals configured deployment ID and matches
`^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,63}$`; `keys` is a non-empty array of at most 16 records.

Every key record has exactly nine required members: `kid`, `state`, `secret_b64url`,
`created_at`, `not_before`, `activated_at`, `verification_only_at`, `verify_until`, and
`retired_at`. No unknown top-level or record member is accepted. Duplicate JSON members
are rejected before object construction; duplicate `kid` or duplicate record is rejected.
`kid` matches the environment-ID regex. A non-null `secret_b64url` is canonical unpadded
base64url decoding to exactly 32 bytes; padding, malformed encoding and other lengths fail.
Only `DESTROYED` requires JSON null secret; every other allowed state requires a secret.

All timestamps are required JSON members and are either JSON null or calendar-valid
`YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ`; offsets, fractions and leap seconds are forbidden. Lifecycle
shapes are exact: `SCHEDULED` has `created_at <= not_before` and all later timestamps null;
`ACTIVE` has `created_at <= not_before <= activated_at <= load_time` and the last three
timestamps null; `VERIFICATION_ONLY` has non-null ordered `created_at <= not_before <=
activated_at <= verification_only_at < verify_until` and `retired_at=null`; `REVOKED` has
non-null activation and retirement, permits no use, and any verification interval ends no
later than retirement; `RETIRED` has non-null activation/retirement and, when present,
`verification_only_at < verify_until <= retired_at`; `DESTROYED` has the revoked shape.
Exactly one key is `ACTIVE` and every other record conforms to its profile subset.

| Setting | `profile_id` const | Purpose | Allowed non-active states / retention |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ADR065_PERSONNEL_PREVIEW_HMAC_KEY_RING_FILE` | `personnel-preview-v1` | token signing/verification | `VERIFICATION_ONLY,RETIRED`; demotion sets `verify_until=verification_only_at+2100s` |
| `ADR065_PERSON_IIN_FINGERPRINT_KEY_RING_FILE` | `adr065-person-iin-fp-v1` | operational IIN fingerprint only | ADR §8.1 `SCHEDULED,VERIFICATION_ONLY,REVOKED,DESTROYED`; a live reference forbids retirement/destruction |
| `ADR065_IDEMPOTENCY_IIN_BINDING_KEY_RING_FILE` | `adr065-idempotency-iin-binding-v1` | replay binding only | `VERIFICATION_ONLY,REVOKED,DESTROYED`; `verify_until` covers every nonterminal operation and retained replay |
| `ADR065_EXPECTED_STATE_HMAC_KEY_RING_FILE` | `adr065-expected-state-sensitive-v1` | protected expected-state fields only | `VERIFICATION_ONLY,RETIRED`; retention is at least 2100s and covers every live preview |

The parser ignores input member order but re-emits the closed object as RFC-8785 JCS with
key records sorted by `kid` for its configuration fingerprint. Production files are
regular non-symlinks owned `root:ubuntu`, mode `0440`, under parent directories with no
group/world write; the deployed service identity is `ubuntu:ubuntu`. Tests use identical
parsing and content validation with ownership adapted only to the isolated test account.
Missing/unreadable file, changed file identity during load, wrong owner/mode, malformed
JSON/base64/time, unknown/duplicate member or `kid`, zero/multiple active, invalid state or
temporal order, and profile/version/environment mismatch fail startup/readiness closed.

Each provider exposes only its own profile and refuses cross-profile lookup. No fallback
to application, JWT/session, encryption, evidence or another ADR-065 key exists. Every
safe reason code is exactly `<PREFIX>_KEY_RING_<SUFFIX>`, where `PREFIX` is one of
`ADR065_PREVIEW | ADR065_IIN_FINGERPRINT | ADR065_IDEMPOTENCY_BINDING |
ADR065_EXPECTED_STATE`, and `SUFFIX` is one of `MISSING | UNREADABLE | MALFORMED |
PROFILE_MISMATCH | ENVIRONMENT_MISMATCH | ACTIVE_CARDINALITY | DUPLICATE_KID |
KEY_NOT_YET_VALID | KEY_EXPIRED | KEY_REVOKED | KEY_RETIRED`.
Responses/logs/readiness contain only that code, never a path, `kid`, parser value, secret,
full IIN, identity fingerprint, verifier or protected-field HMAC.

The preview key signs only. Token TTL is exactly 1800 seconds using transaction DB time;
future skew is 300 seconds, `issued_at <= now+300`, validity requires
`issued_at <= now < expires_at`, and previous-key verification ends at `verify_until`,
exactly 2100 seconds after demotion to verification-only (equality is expired). Retirement
occurs no earlier than that instant. Unknown/retired kids are not retried with another key.
The ADR maximum sizes and exact two unpadded base64url segments are enforced.

**Transaction.** The same read-only preflight transaction; all participating rows are read
from one snapshot; token signing occurs only after the snapshot has produced the exact JCS
state object; rollback ends the transaction.

**Invariants.** ORCH-19–ORCH-21, ORCH-33, ORCH-40, ORCH-43–ORCH-48. The state includes the
Employee row/NULL link, Employee identities, complete P0 candidate absence, assignment
scope/absence, org/position references, watermark, evidence generation/collections and
full import provenance selection.

**Errors.** `ACTIVE_STATE_*`, `ORDER_EVIDENCE_SCOPE_INVALID`,
`POSITION_NAME_MISMATCH`, token/profile/key errors, and input completeness errors occur
before token release.

**Tests.** Real API tests cover both routes, all response material, exact byte echo,
server-side recomputation, missing/extra/reordered/altered confirmation fields, and prove
that copied client digest/fingerprint/verifier values have no authority. Eight separate
parameterized cases change exactly org unit, position, rate, employment type,
`is_primary`, start date, evidence or reason between prepare and confirm; each returns
`PREVIEW_CONFIRMATION_MISMATCH`, no token and no write. Tests cover complete P0 intention,
deterministic JCS and every request/state digest member; actor, role/context, environment,
target, correlation, import, watermark and protected-field mismatch; confirmation replay,
TTL/not-before/expiry and token replay/tampering. For each ring parser tests cover valid
minimal/rotation files, missing/unknown/duplicate member, duplicate `kid`, zero/multiple
active, malformed base64/length, wrong profile/version/environment, invalid timestamp
format/order, expired verification-only, retired/unknown key, missing/unreadable file,
cross-profile lookup and rotation boundaries. Logs, exceptions, tokens, readiness and
metric labels are asserted free of secrets, full IIN and guessable derivatives. A
production-path SQL listener and before/after snapshots prove no DML, migration DDL,
`nextval`/`setval`, ORM flush, commit, audit, timestamp mutation, apply route or hidden
write on every prepare/confirm success, blocked and error exit.

**Ready when.** Step 1 returns all safe confirmation material; an exact step-2 echo yields a
reproducible complete P0 token/digests while `apply_available=false`; changed material,
authorization, input or state fails closed. This slice contains only the two read-only
routes, schemas, services, four providers, configuration/readiness and tests. It contains
no DML, migration, apply route, frontend or production enablement.

### Slice 2 — Apply schema and database guarantees

**Purpose.** Install the exact persisted contracts required by ADR-065 §§4, 5, 8 and 11,
without exposing an apply endpoint.

**Existing files:**

- `alembic/versions/l9m0n1o2p3q4_adr065_preflight_schema_dependencies.py` — read-only
  baseline reference; do not rewrite its deployed contract
- `app/db/models/personnel_migration.py`
- `app/services/security_audit_service.py`
- `tests/db_guard.py`

**Exact new migration chain on the clean ADR baseline:**

1. `alembic/versions/m0n1o2p3q4r5_adr065_apply_core.py`,
   `down_revision=l9m0n1o2p3q4`: operation/idempotency, assignment provenance,
   application keys, events/audit/import binding, unique and exclusion guarantees;
2. `alembic/versions/n1o2p3q4r5s6_adr065_boundary_runtime.py`,
   `down_revision=m0n1o2p3q4r5`: the complete §11.3 boundary-run schema, functions,
   triggers, role and grants;
3. `alembic/versions/o2p3q4r5s6t7_adr065_delivery_and_onboarding_idempotency.py`,
   `down_revision=n1o2p3q4r5s6`: delivery-attempt/ACK persistence and the §9.3.2
   onboarding retry identities required before boundary/apply writer enablement.

These IDs are reserved by this WP and must be rechecked for collision on the clean
implementation branch. The unrelated dirty `k8` revision is neither a parent nor a
package input. The three migrations may not be combined: catalog ownership, guarded
downgrade and rollout evidence are independently reviewed.

**New tests:**

- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_apply_operation_schema.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_assignment_guarantees.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_event_audit_schema.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_boundary_runtime_migration.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_delivery_ack_runtime.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_adr065_onboarding_retry_identity.py`

**Schema outputs.** Exact `personnel_orchestration_operations`; request/person-resolution
checks; ADR-065 assignment provenance and `row_version`; immutable provenance/adoption
trigger; event columns/checks/coupling guards; strict audit vocabulary; required FKs and
indexes.

**Complete boundary-run output.** The second migration creates
`person_assignment_boundary_runs` with the exact ADR §11.3 columns: generated
`boundary_run_id`, unique scheduler run UUID/context fingerprint, target date, STARTED or
COMPLETED status, attempt/lease/T1/completion timestamps, lease owner and recovery count,
closed outcome, projection consistency, before/after watermark date/generation,
`service_actor`, optional requester, retry parent/ordinal, closed error metadata, and
`updated_at`. It installs every named CHECK, the single retry-child partial unique index,
immutable terminal/DELETE and retry-lineage triggers, insert-shape guard, and deferred
T1-commit guard. It creates only the ADR functions
`claim_person_assignment_boundary_run`,
`begin_person_assignment_boundary_run_t1`,
`recover_person_assignment_boundary_run`, and
`finalize_person_assignment_boundary_run` as `SECURITY DEFINER` with fixed search path,
plus metadata validators. Direct DML is revoked.

The migration creates NOLOGIN role `adr065_boundary_runtime`; deployment grants it only
to the C2 worker runtime identity. That identity has SELECT plus EXECUTE on the four
functions, never table DML. Claim is idempotent by `scheduler_run_id` and immutable
context; conflicting reuse fails. Recovery changes only an expired STARTED/T0 lease and
increments `recovery_count`; a run after T1 starts cannot commit incomplete because the
deferred guard aborts it. Retry is a single child of a completed retryable failure and
must preserve date/context/actor/requester with ordinal +1.

**C2 runtime ownership.** Extend
`app/services/hr_person_assignment_sync_service.py::assignment_boundary_activation_tx`
only as a compatibility shell over new
`app/services/person_assignment_boundary_runner_service.py::run_boundary` and
`app/services/person_assignment_boundary_run_repository.py`. The runner owns T0 claim
and a separate caller-owned T1 transaction, takes exclusive lock class 1a before evidence
rows, calls begin, validates lifecycle successor/current-active-primary state, performs
C2 assignment/projection/reconciliation changes, persists delivery/ACK prerequisites,
advances the watermark last, appends the boundary audit, and finalizes the run in that same
T1. No success watermark can exist without matching COMPLETED
`BOUNDARY_RUN_ADVANCED` evidence. Cancellation before T1 is a terminal T0 operation;
crash recovery/retry follows only the functions above.

**Delivery/ACK prerequisites.** Extend
`app/employee_onboarding/infrastructure/notification_repository.py::create_onboarding_notification_tx`,
`app/employee_onboarding/infrastructure/notification_repository.py::list_pending_onboarding_deliveries`,
`app/employee_onboarding/infrastructure/notification_repository.py::ack_onboarding_delivery`,
and
`app/directory/employee_onboarding_internal_routes.py` to use the ADR §9.3.1 current
attempt state machine and runtime role `adr065_delivery_runtime`. ACK is conditional on
the claimed attempt/lease/digest and persists before any boundary result that depends on
delivery. The third migration installs exact delivery state/checks/functions/grants and
the §9.3.2 unique scope/key/digest columns for production-like onboarding INSERT writers.
Unmigrated notification, delivery, ACK, reminder or onboarding routes stay disabled.

**Hard assignment guarantees.** Create exactly:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_pa_one_current_active_primary_per_person
  ON public.person_assignments (person_id)
  WHERE is_primary = TRUE
    AND active_flag = TRUE
    AND lifecycle_status = 'active';
```

and the ADR-065 exclusion constraint `ex_pa_primary_period_no_overlap` over the inclusive
date range for `is_primary=TRUE AND lifecycle_status<>'voided'`. The partial unique index
is not a substitute for the exclusion constraint; advisory locks are not a substitute for
either database guarantee.

**Data preflight.** Report and stop on duplicate active primaries, overlapping non-VOID
periods, duplicate assignment/application keys, invalid provenance, inconsistent event
references or unsupported audit types. The migration must not repair or delete them.

**Boundary/data preflight and downgrade.** Before DDL, assert no incompatible legacy
assignment state, no active boundary runtime session/role membership, and no conflicting
delivery/onboarding retry state. Upgrade order is `m0` → `n1` → `o2`; writers remain
disabled until all catalog and runtime gates pass. Downgrade of `o2` refuses retained
attempt/idempotency evidence. Downgrade of `n1` first proves the runtime role disabled and
`person_assignment_boundary_runs` empty; any STARTED or COMPLETED row is retained
evidence and causes refusal—never silent delete/export. It then revokes grants/functions,
drops triggers/functions/table/validators in ADR order, and drops the role. `m0`
downgrade retains the existing forward-fix rule once operation/provenance data exists.

**Tests.** Upgrade/downgrade and chain order; exact PostgreSQL catalog definitions, function
security/search paths, triggers, checks and runtime ACLs; claim/replay/conflicting reuse,
lifecycle transitions, lease recovery, single retry child and concurrent runs; incomplete
T1 commit failure; watermark advancement only after valid finalization and delivery/ACK
prerequisites; duplicate, crash-recovery, audit failure and finalization failure; rollback
with no partial assignment/projection/watermark change; startup failure for incomplete or
failed current boundary evidence; plus the existing NULL/check/FK, unique/exclusion,
legacy-preflight and guarded-downgrade tests.

**Ready when.** Catalog tests match ADR literally; migrations contain no endpoint enablement;
an invalid baseline fails before DDL; apply remains disabled.

### Slice 3 — Idempotency operation and locked stale-state protocol

**Purpose.** Implement ADR-065 §8 and the apply-side §7 reread without invoking domain
writers.

**Existing files:**

- `app/directory/personnel_lk_schemas.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_expected_state.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_preview_token.py`
- `app/db/engine.py`

**New files:**

- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_idempotency.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_locked_state.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_idempotency.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_locked_state.py`

**Inputs.** Raw idempotency key only in transient authenticated input; actor/context;
correlation UUID; exact business request; trusted preview token.

**Outputs.** An uncommitted `operation_id`, `P0_CREATE|P1_ADOPT` resolution code, or an
authorized committed replay. Generated `operation_id` is not part of the pre-operation
business digest.

**Transaction.** One caller-owned write-capable transaction. Authentication,
authorization and `REQUEST_COMPLETENESS` precede operation lookup/INSERT. After a replay
miss, acquire the operation lock, insert/resolve the operation row, acquire the complete
§9 lock set, rebuild the state digest and stop before domain DML. Every non-replay exit
rolls back the operation row.

**Errors.** `ASSIGNMENT_INTENT_INCOMPLETE`, `IDEMPOTENCY_IDENTITY_INPUT_CONFLICT`, binding
key errors, `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED`, `IDEMPOTENCY_OPERATION_INCOMPLETE`, token errors,
`STALE_EXPECTED_STATE` and the more specific active-state/position errors in ADR precedence.

**Tests.** Same key/same digest, same key/different digest, concurrent same-key requests,
rollback then retry, unknown commit retry, replay after token expiry, actor/context
separation, raw-IIN verifier mismatch, and proof that no domain table changes in this slice.

**Ready when.** Replays and conflicts are deterministic and no code can proceed beyond the
locked-state gate because no domain dispatcher is registered.

### Slice 4 — Caller-owned authority ports

**Purpose.** Provide the only legal write boundaries used later by the composite. This
slice adds no public route.

| Authority | Existing files to change | New file, if separation is required | Required port/result |
|---|---|---|---|
| ADR-048 resolution/Create-or-Link | `app/services/adr048_person_resolution_service.py` | `app/services/adr048_person_create_or_link_service.py` | caller connection + held identity lock; create P0 Shell or adopt P1 Person; no transaction ownership |
| Employee identity/link | `app/services/adr048_person_create_or_link_service.py`, `app/db/models/employee_identity.py` | none | exact-IIN save/verify without overwrite; set only expected NULL `employees.person_id` |
| ADR-043/C2 | `app/services/hr_person_assignment_sync_service.py` | no second lifecycle engine | `execute_assignment_command_tx(conn, command)`; strict `OPEN_ASSIGNMENT`; no fallback/default/date inference |
| Reconciliation | `app/services/assignment_reconciliation_service.py` | none | `reconcile_employee_primary_assignment_tx(conn, expected_employee_id, expected_person_id, expected_assignment_id_or_null)` |
| Personnel event | `app/services/personnel_record_event_service.py` | none | strict caller-connection P0/P1 event bundle with returned IDs |
| Success audit | `app/services/security_audit_service.py` | none | strict caller-connection success audit; missing audit ID is fatal |
| Import binding/provenance | `app/services/hr_import_employee_binding_service.py`, `app/services/hr_import_enroll_employee_service.py` | `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_import_binding.py` only as an orchestrator adapter | exact selected rows/records, operation FK, stable order; no inferred selection |
| Contact projection | `app/services/operational_contact_service.py::{find_operational_contact_id,ensure_operational_contact_for_employee}` | none; both already accept caller `Connection`; use `ensure_operational_contact_for_employee` only after class-13 Contact lock and never call directory Contact route writers | last projection; no identity authority |

The ADR-048 write port must call the existing exact-IIN decision rules; it must not create a
second candidate resolver. The C2 port must reuse canonical C2 lifecycle primitives; it
must not copy assignment SQL into `personnel_lk`.

**Application assignment identity.** C2 computes RFC-8785 JCS over exactly
`version,person_id,employment_type,is_primary,org_unit_id,position_id,start_date`, then:

```text
business_identity_hash = lowercase_hex(SHA-256(jcs_bytes))
application_assignment_key = "app:v1:" + business_identity_hash
```

The application key, business hash, operation origin, evidence fingerprint and lineage are
immutable. Exact-key or semantic adoption follows only ADR-065 §4.3 under the Person
assignment-scope lock. Ambiguous, incompatible, stale or differently owned candidates
fail closed; a voided assignment is not adoptable.

**Tests.** Unit tests for each port, caller-connection proofs, no nested commit/rollback,
P0 create and rollback, P1 adoption/no duplicate, exact identity conflict, C2 no-default
guards, assignment key vectors, exact/semantic adoption, ambiguity, replay, concurrency,
reconciliation expected-ID mismatch, event/audit failures and import ownership conflicts.

**Ready when.** Each port passes independently with the caller controlling commit and all
legacy transaction-owning wrappers remain outside the composite call graph.

### Slice 5 — Atomic composite P0 application service

**Purpose.** Assemble the approved authorities into one transaction without exposing UI.

**New files:**

- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_apply_service.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_locking.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_apply_service.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_apply_concurrency.py`
- `tests/personnel_lk/test_control_list_repair_apply_fault_injection.py`

**Existing files:** all Slice 3–4 ports and `app/db/engine.py`.

**Inputs.** Trusted closed request, preview token, idempotency key, correlation, actor and
verifier context. The service rederives classification; caller-supplied classification,
mode or outcome is never authoritative.

**One transaction, exact order.** After auth/completeness/idempotency and locked stale-state
validation:

1. ADR-048 creates the P0 Person Shell;
2. ADR-048 link/identity port verifies and sets the existing Employee link/IIN;
3. strict C2 creates the primary assignment or performs only the deterministic adoption
   allowed by ADR-065;
4. assignment link and reconciliation project the returned assignment ID;
5. P0 events are appended exactly `PERSON_SHELL_CREATED`, then
   `EMPLOYEE_PERSON_LINKED`;
6. strict success audit is appended;
7. exact import binding, normalized propagation and import metadata are persisted;
8. Contact is projected;
9. operation result becomes `SUCCEEDED`;
10. the caller commits once.

Every assertion failure raises to the transaction owner. There is no compensation path.
Before commit the final postcondition is exactly one compatible Person, the target Employee
linked to it, matching identity, exactly one returned non-VOID primary with confirmed
terms, matching reconciliation, required events/audit/provenance/bindings and safe result.

**Prohibited state.** `employees.person_id IS NOT NULL` with the required assignment absent
may exist only as an uncommitted intermediate row. No error handler, audit attempt or
retry may commit it.

**Fault tests.** One transaction per physical statement ordinal at every applicable
ADR-065 FI boundary: Shell, link, identity validation/write, assignment/provenance,
assignment link, reconciliation, both personnel events, audit, each import binding,
each normalized update, separate import metadata, Contact and result finalization. After
each injection the snapshot equals the pre-operation committed state and retry starts
fresh with the same key/request.

**Ready when.** The P0 service passes success, race, retry and all physical-boundary rollback
tests and remains unreachable from a public endpoint.

### Slice 6 — Operator API/application boundary

**Purpose.** Expose the atomic service only after backend gates pass.

**Existing files:**

- `app/directory/personnel_lk_routes.py`
- `app/directory/personnel_lk_schemas.py`
- `app/personnel_lk/application/control_list_repair_preflight_service.py`

**New/extended API.** Add `POST /directory/personnel/lk/control-list-repair/apply` only in
this slice. It uses `require_personnel_admin_or_403`, accepts the trusted-token/idempotency
contract and the same closed business request, and returns the safe stored result. It does
not accept an authoritative P0/P1, mode or outcome.

Add the personnel-admin protected read-only
`GET /directory/personnel/lk/control-list-repair/readiness` in the same route file. It
returns only enabled/disabled plus stable reason codes from
`adr065_readiness_service.py`; it exposes no key IDs, secrets, IIN, SQL or row payloads.
The apply route recomputes the same gate at request start, so hiding the UI cannot bypass
it.

**Transaction.** The route opens exactly one caller-owned transaction and delegates to
`control_list_repair_apply_service`; domain ports use the same `Connection`. Commit occurs
only after the final result, and every exception path rolls back before mapping the stable
response.

**Errors.** Machine-readable stable codes preserve ADR precedence. SQL statements, bind
parameters, full IIN, keys, protected evidence and exception strings do not enter HTTP
responses or logs.

**Tests.** Real FastAPI route, auth/permission, request validation/redaction, success P0,
replay, conflict, stale token/state, structural blockers, reason/evidence compatibility,
incomplete decisions, DB exceptions after a real business SELECT, and persisted snapshot
proof for every response path.

**Ready when.** API tests prove one result/commit, no partial state and no alternate internal
route bypasses permission or transaction ownership. Apply remains feature-disabled until
Slice 8.

### Slice 7 — Operator UI: `+ Из контр. списка`

**Route and placement.** The button belongs in the page header of
`/directory/personnel/journal`, implemented by:

`ImportEnrollEmployeeWizard.tsx`, org-unit/position option hooks already used in personnel
forms, common API helpers from `@/lib/api`, and `buildEmployeeCardHref` for post-success
navigation. Do not reuse import-derived defaulting from the enrollment wizard.

**Workflow.** Enter exact IIN → choose exact import provenance → display Employee,
Person/link, identity and assignment preflight read-only → display backend classification
→ explicitly enter/confirm all eight decisions → call prepare and display the safe
server-generated P0 confirmation material → explicit operator confirmation → echo the
opaque material through confirm and receive the preview token → apply → refresh journal
and open/refresh the card only after success.

**No inference.** Org unit, position, rate, employment type, primary flag, start date,
evidence admissibility/reference and reason begin empty. Import, Employee projection, HIRE
event and order may be displayed as read-only context only. A personnel-order date never
prefills `start_date`; selecting an order never implies admissibility.

**Tests.** Button placement/permission, exact-IIN validation, P0 display, P1/blocker display,
eight empty inputs, each missing/invalid field, no import/Employee/HIRE/order inference,
stale refresh, conflict/retry UX, double-submit idempotency, success refresh, masked IIN and
safe errors.

**Ready when.** Apply is impossible until all fields and backend preview are complete;
double-click/reload cannot create duplicates; UI never predicts success from the current
PPR `409`.

### Slice 8 — Writer compatibility and rollout gate

**Purpose.** Prove that every concurrent writer follows ADR-065 §9 before enabling apply.
The following 594-row matrix is the closed baseline inventory. Rows W001–W130 are a
one-for-one transfer of the 130 physical data rows in the ADR-065 §9.3 writer table
(header/separator excluded). W131–W594 are 464 unique callable, migration, Contact,
security, script/CLI and production-like test/fixture expansions. The independent
lexical-owner/data-flow recount proves 757 distinct direct-DML
`(file,qualified_callable,SQL_verb,target_relation)` pairs for the closed ADR relation set:
uncovered `0`, duplicate callable/DML pairs `0`, false-positive pairs `0`. Nested functions
are scoped by AST ranges, so outer DML is attributed to the outer callable; executed SQL
arguments and SQLAlchemy mutation constructors count, while imports, comments,
declarations, read-only access and string assertions do not. Upgrade and downgrade are
separate callable identities. “Migrate” means the exact callable adopts
the common lock/transaction/idempotency protocol before its entrypoint is enabled.
“Disable” means removal from the production package plus a route/command/startup denial;
commentary or operator convention is insufficient.

| Measure | Revision-4 count/evidence |
|---|---|
